## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `ecd83012c69539b3e700f0baf806b4bec73fac1aaaec8b7ff68669bc262ee38c`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-teachers-tell-if-a-student-is-copying-from-another-device` (Similarity: 0.9293)

```text
Yes. Plagiarism checkers and writing analytics tools detect text copied from another screen just as effectively as text copied and pasted directly via the clipboard.

A widespread misconception among students is that plagiarism detectors monitor the computer's "copy/paste clipboard." Consequently, some students place a phone, tablet, or secondary monitor next to their laptop and manually type out an online article, Wikipedia page, or ChatGPT response word-for-word, believing that physical retyping breaks the digital connection.

In reality, plagiarism algorithms evaluate the final submitted text strings against global databases—regardless of how the letters were entered. Furthermore, through Checkmark Plagiarism's Transcription + Source Matching , the physical keystroke rhythm of reading from a second screen is recorded and exposed.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers source matching by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: Why Retyping from Another Screen Fails to Beat Plagiarism Checkers
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `9c175d634013c28fdd1def8086ea62673000e6b634b341ca5342a1c0f22ee955`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-students-use-ai-to-hide-plagiarism` (Similarity: 0.8769)

```text
1. String & N-Gram Matching

Plagiarism engines compare 8-word to 16-word sequences (n-grams) against billions of web pages. The resulting text match is 100% identical regardless of typing method.

2. Transcription Keystroke Rhythm

Looking back and forth at a phone creates a distinctive gaze-shift pause pattern (type 5 words → pause 2 sec → type 5 words) with near-zero backspacing.

3. Cross-Institutional Peer Indexing

If the text is copied from a classmate's phone, institutional repository matching identifies the exact sentence structures across both submissions.

4. AI Detection Remains Active

If the secondary screen displays ChatGPT, retyping the response by hand does not change its statistical perplexity, burstiness, or hallucinated citations.
```

### Section: The Dual Trap: What Checkmark Analysis Reveals
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `2dbe74270e7b1011b1c3598ffcba149f0223a4663670ad86f94c434aebbc96ed`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/why-shouldnt-teachers-rely-on-an-ai-percentage-alone` (Similarity: 0.9058)

```text
When a student types text from a secondary screen, they trigger two simultaneous detection layers in Checkmark Plagiarism :

The Linguistic Layer: If the text came from a website, Checkmark highlights the exact matching URL. If it came from AI, the AI detector flags low burstiness and signature cliches. 
 The Behavioral Layer: Essay Writing Playback reveals that the entire paper was typed linearly from start to finish without a single sentence deletion or major revision.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```


### Section: A 5-Step Educator Investigation Protocol
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `baaac8af51e91d774dea3573298efad181836f5a2a450accebbfa7da7e5430ae`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-students-avoid-plagiarism-detection-by-retyping-an-article` (Similarity: 0.8978)

```text
Educator Cross-Screen Audit Checklist:

1. Open the Checkmark Plagiarism report to review overall direct match percentages. 
 2. Click highlighted source links to view matching web pages side-by-side. 
 3. Open Essay Playback to check the student's typing cadence and backspace rate. 
 4. If typing is completely linear with 0% edits, transcription from a second screen is confirmed. 
 5. Hold a supportive conference asking the student to explain how the text was composed.
```
